# Asset log

Every file under `art/` and `audio/` needs a row here (checked by `tools/ip_check.sh`).
Code-drawn art (`PixelFont`, UI frames, battle backdrops, title illustration) is generated at runtime and has no files.
Audio under `audio/` is produced by `tools/audio/gen_audio.py` (needs numpy and ffmpeg); re-run it to rebuild.
The Era I PNGs below are produced by the scripts in `tools/art/` from hand-written shapes and pixel rows; re-run them to rebuild the art.
